Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a immunology algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 44. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 56/5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 44 + O(1/n^56). Summing over all modes and applying the immunology convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
